```java
package dev.guardx;

import dev.guardx.util.Compat;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.command.Command;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.command.TabCompleter;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.OfflinePlayer;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class GuardCommand implements CommandExecutor, TabCompleter {

    private static final List<String> SUB = List.of("help", "reload", "alerts", "debug", "info", "reset",
            "diagnostics", "checks", "inspect", "report", "reports", "close", "history", "dump", "goto", "top", "exempt", "stats", "layers", "notifytest", "netcheck", "netrevoke", "netlist");
    private static final List<String> REASONS = List.of("killaura", "fly", "speed", "reach", "autoclicker", "scaffold", "velocity", "other");
    private static final DateTimeFormatter FILE_TIME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss");
    private final GuardX plugin;

    public GuardCommand(GuardX plugin) { this.plugin = plugin; }

    private String msg(String key, Object... kv) {
        return plugin.lang().msg(key, kv);
    }

    private String tr(String key, Object... kv) {
        return plugin.lang().tr(key, kv);
    }

    private boolean admin(CommandSender sender) {
        if (sender.hasPermission("guardx.admin")) return true;
        sender.sendMessage(msg("no-permission"));
        return false;
    }

    private Player findPlayer(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        Player target = args.length > 1 ? Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]) : null;
        if (target == null) sender.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found"));
        return target;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender s, Command cmd, String label, String[] args) {
        // /report <игрок> <причина> — короткий алиас для жалобы
        if (cmd.getName().equalsIgnoreCase("report")) {
            String[] shifted = new String[args.length + 1];
            shifted[0] = "report";
            System.arraycopy(args, 0, shifted, 1, args.length);
            args = shifted;
        }
        String sub = args.length == 0 ? "help" : args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        switch (sub) {
            case "reload" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                plugin.reloadAll();
                s.sendMessage(msg("reloaded"));
            }
            case "alerts" -> {
                if (!(s instanceof Player p)) { s.sendMessage(tr("players-only")); return true; }
                if (!p.hasPermission("guardx.alerts")) { s.sendMessage(msg("no-permission")); return true; }
                boolean on = plugin.violations().toggleAlerts(p.getUniqueId());
                s.sendMessage(msg(on ? "alerts-on" : "alerts-off"));
            }
            case "debug" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (!(s instanceof Player p)) { s.sendMessage(tr("players-only")); return true; }
                s.sendMessage(msg(plugin.violations().toggleDebug(p.getUniqueId()) ? "debug-on" : "debug-off"));
            }
            case "info" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                Map<String, Double> vl = plugin.violations().get(t.getUniqueId());
                s.sendMessage(tr("info-title", "player", t.getName()));
                boolean any = false;
                for (Map.Entry<String, Double> en : vl.entrySet()) {
                    if (en.getValue() <= 0) continue;
                    any = true;
                    s.sendMessage(tr("vl-line", "check", en.getKey(), "vl", Lang.n(en.getValue(), 1)));
                }
                if (!any) s.sendMessage(tr("no-violations"));
            }
            case "inspect" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                Location loc = t.getLocation();
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-title", "player", t.getName()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-uuid", "value", t.getUniqueId()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-ping", "ping", Compat.ping(t), "mode", t.getGameMode()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-world", "value", t.getWorld().getName()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-pos", "x", Lang.n(loc.getX(), 2), "y", Lang.n(loc.getY(), 2), "z", Lang.n(loc.getZ(), 2)));
                s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-health", "hp", Lang.n(t.getHealth(), 1), "food", t.getFoodLevel()));
                Map<String, Double> vl = plugin.violations().get(t.getUniqueId());
                if (vl.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-vl-none"));
                else vl.entrySet().stream().filter(e -> e.getValue() > 0)
                        .sorted(Map.Entry.<String, Double>comparingByValue().reversed())
                        .forEach(e -> s.sendMessage(tr("vl-line", "check", e.getKey(), "vl", Lang.n(e.getValue(), 1))));
                int open = plugin.reports().openFor(t.getName()).size();
                if (open > 0) s.sendMessage(tr("inspect-open-reports", "count", open)
                        + (plugin.reports().isSuspect(t.getUniqueId()) ? tr("inspect-suspect") : ""));
            }
            case "report" -> {
                // Админ без причины: старое поведение — сохранить подробный отчёт в файл
                if (args.length == 2 && s.hasPermission("guardx.admin")) {
                    dump(s, args);
                    return true;
                }
                if (!(s instanceof Player reporter)) { s.sendMessage(tr("players-only")); return true; }
                if (!reporter.hasPermission("guardx.report")) { s.sendMessage(msg("no-permission")); return true; }
                if (args.length < 3) {
                    s.sendMessage(msg("report-usage"));
                    return true;
                }
                Player t = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);
                if (t == null) { s.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found")); return true; }
                String reason = String.join(" ", java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(args, 2, args.length));
                switch (plugin.reports().submit(reporter, t, reason)) {
                    case OK -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-sent", "player", t.getName()));
                    case SELF -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-self"));
                    case COOLDOWN -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-cooldown"));
                    case DUPLICATE -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-duplicate"));
                    case NO_REASON -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-no-reason"));
                    case EXEMPT -> s.sendMessage(msg("report-exempt"));
                }
            }
            case "reports" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                java.util.List<ReportManager.Report> open = plugin.reports().open();
                if (open.isEmpty()) { s.sendMessage(tr("reports-none")); return true; }
                int page = 1;
                if (args.length > 1) {
                    try { page = Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(args[1])); } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { }
                }
                int pages = (open.size() + 7) / 8;
                page = Math.min(page, pages);
                s.sendMessage(tr("reports-title", "count", open.size(), "page", page, "pages", pages));
                for (int i = (page - 1) * 8; i < Math.min(open.size(), page * 8); i++) {
                    ReportManager.Report r = open.get(i);
                    int count = plugin.reports().distinctReporters(r.target);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("reports-line", "id", r.id, "target", r.targetName, "from", tr("reports-from"),
                            "reporter", r.reporterName, "reason", r.reason, "vl", Lang.n(r.totalVl, 0),
                            "count", count > 1 ? tr("reports-count", "count", count) : ""));
                }
                s.sendMessage(tr("reports-footer"));
            }
            case "close" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (args.length < 2) { s.sendMessage(tr("close-usage")); return true; }
                int closed = 0;
                java.util.List<ReportManager.Report> toNotify = new ArrayList<>();
                try {
                    ReportManager.Report r = plugin.reports().byId(Integer.parseInt(args[1]));
                    if (r != null && plugin.reports().close(r)) { closed = 1; toNotify.add(r); }
                } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
                    toNotify.addAll(plugin.reports().openFor(args[1]));
                    closed = plugin.reports().closeAllFor(args[1]);
                }
                if (closed == 0) { s.sendMessage(tr("close-not-found")); return true; }
                s.sendMessage(tr("close-done", "count", closed));
                // Благодарим тех, кто пожаловался
                for (ReportManager.Report r : toNotify) {
                    if (r.auto()) continue;
                    Player who = Bukkit.getPlayer(r.reporter);
                    if (who != null) who.sendMessage(msg("report-closed", "player", r.targetName));
                }
            }
            case "history" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (args.length < 2) { s.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found")); return true; }
                Player online = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);
                UUID id = online != null ? online.getUniqueId() : plugin.history().find(args[1]);
                if (id == null) { s.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found")); return true; }
                String name = online != null ? online.getName() : args[1];
                var all = new ArrayList<>(plugin.history().entries(id, plugin.history().maxEntries()));
                java.util.Collections.reverse(all); // новые первыми
                int pages = Math.max(1, (all.size() + 9) / 10);
                int page = 1;
                if (args.length > 2) {
                    try { page = Integer.parseInt(args[2]); } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { page = 1; }
                }
                page = Math.max(1, Math.min(page, pages));
                s.sendMessage(tr("history-title", "player", name));
                if (all.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("no-violations"));
                for (int i = (page - 1) * 10; i < Math.min(all.size(), page * 10); i++) {
                    var e = all.get(i);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("history-line", "time", HistoryStore.time(e), "check", e.check(),
                            "info", e.info(), "vl", Lang.n(e.vl(), 1)));
                }
                if (pages > 1) s.sendMessage(tr("history-footer", "page", page, "pages", pages, "player", name));
            }
            case "layers" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                var all = plugin.layers().all();
                s.sendMessage(tr("layers-title"));
                if (all.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("layers-none"));
                String any = tr("layers-all");
                for (var l : all) {
                    String area = l.min() == null ? any : String.format(Locale.US, "%.0f,%.0f,%.0f → %.0f,%.0f,%.0f",
                            l.min()[0], l.min()[1], l.min()[2], l.max()[0], l.max()[1], l.max()[2]);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("layers-line", "name", l.name(),
                            "worlds", l.worlds().isEmpty() ? any : String.join(", ", l.worlds()),
                            "checks", l.allChecks() ? any : String.join(", ", l.checks()),
                            "area", area, "permission", l.permission() == null ? any : l.permission()));
                }
                if (args.length > 1) {
                    Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                    var active = plugin.layers().activeFor(t);
                    if (active.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("layers-active-none", "player", t.getName()));
                    else s.sendMessage(tr("layers-active", "player", t.getName(),
                            "layers", String.join(", ", active.stream().map(dev.guardx.LayerManager.Layer::name).toList())));
                }
            }
            case "notifytest" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                List<String> sent = plugin.notifications().test();
                if (sent.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("notifytest-none"));
                else s.sendMessage(tr("notifytest-sent", "channels", String.join(", ", sent)));
            }
            case "netcheck" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (args.length < 2) { s.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found")); return true; }
                if (!plugin.network().enabled()) { s.sendMessage(tr("network-disabled")); return true; }
                Player online = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);
                String shown = online != null ? online.getName() : args[1];
                plugin.network().lookup(online != null ? online.getUniqueId() : null, shown, false, found -> {
                    if (found == null) { s.sendMessage(tr("network-error")); return; }
                    s.sendMessage(tr("network-title", "player", shown));
                    if (found.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("network-none"));
                    for (var r : found) {
                        s.sendMessage(tr("network-line", "time", NetworkStore.time(r.time()), "server", r.server(),
                                "check", r.check(), "info", r.info(), "vl", Lang.n(r.vl(), 1)));
                    }
                });
            }
            case "netrevoke" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (args.length < 2) { s.sendMessage(msg("player-not-found")); return true; }
                if (!plugin.network().enabled()) { s.sendMessage(tr("network-disabled")); return true; }
                Player online = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);
                String shown = online != null ? online.getName() : args[1];
                plugin.network().revoke(online != null ? online.getUniqueId() : null, shown, changed -> {
                    if (changed < 0) s.sendMessage(tr("network-error"));
                    else if (changed == 0) s.sendMessage(tr("network-revoke-none", "player", shown));
                    else s.sendMessage(tr("network-revoked", "player", shown, "count", changed));
                });
            }
            case "netlist" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (!plugin.network().enabled()) { s.sendMessage(tr("network-disabled")); return true; }
                int requested = 1;
                if (args.length > 1) {
                    try { requested = Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(args[1])); } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) { }
                }
                final int reqPage = requested;
                final int size = 8;
                plugin.network().listRecent((reqPage - 1) * size, size, result -> {
                    if (result == null) { s.sendMessage(tr("network-error")); return; }
                    int pages = Math.max(1, (result.total() + size - 1) / size);
                    int page = Math.min(reqPage, pages);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("netlist-title", "total", result.total(), "page", page, "pages", pages));
                    if (result.rows().isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("network-none"));
                    for (var r : result.rows()) {
                        s.sendMessage(tr("network-line", "time", NetworkStore.time(r.time()), "server", r.server(),
                                "check", r.check(), "info", r.info(), "vl", Lang.n(r.vl(), 1)));
                    }
                    s.sendMessage(tr("netlist-footer", "page", page, "pages", pages));
                });
            }
            case "dump" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                dump(s, args);
            }
            case "goto" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                if (!(s instanceof Player admin)) { s.sendMessage(tr("players-only")); return true; }
                Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                admin.teleport(t.getLocation());
                s.sendMessage(tr("goto-done", "player", t.getName()));
            }
            case "top" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                s.sendMessage(tr("top-title"));
                List<Map.Entry<Player, Double>> rows = new ArrayList<>();
                for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                    double total = plugin.violations().get(player.getUniqueId()).values().stream()
                            .filter(Double::isFinite).mapToDouble(v -> Math.max(0, v)).sum();
                    if (total > 0) rows.add(Map.entry(player, total));
                }
                rows.sort(Map.Entry.<Player, Double>comparingByValue().reversed());
                if (rows.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("top-none"));
                for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(10, rows.size()); i++) {
                    var row = rows.get(i);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("top-line", "n", i + 1, "player", row.getKey().getName(), "vl", Lang.n(row.getValue(), 1)));
                }
            }
            case "diagnostics" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-title"));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-players", "value", Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().size()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-packets", "value", plugin.packetLayer() == null ? "-" : plugin.packetLayer().name()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-checks", "value", plugin.checks().all().size()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-calls", "value", plugin.checks().calls()));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-avg", "value", String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", plugin.checks().averageMicros())));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-max", "value", String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", plugin.checks().maxMicros())));
                s.sendMessage(tr("diag-errors", "value", plugin.checks().brokenCount()));
                if (!plugin.checks().brokenChecks().isEmpty())
                    s.sendMessage(tr("diag-broken", "value", String.join(", ", plugin.checks().brokenChecks())));
            }
            case "checks" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                s.sendMessage(tr("checks-title"));
                for (var check : plugin.checks().all()) {
                    boolean enabled = check.enabled();
                    s.sendMessage((enabled ? ChatColor.GREEN : ChatColor.RED) + check.id()
                            + tr(enabled ? "checks-enabled" : "checks-disabled"));
                }
            }
            case "exempt" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                if (args.length > 2 && args[2].equalsIgnoreCase("off")) {
                    plugin.setExempt(t, 0L);
                    s.sendMessage(tr("exempt-off", "player", t.getName()));
                    return true;
                }
                int minutes = 10;
                if (args.length > 2) {
                    try {
                        minutes = Integer.parseInt(args[2]);
                    } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
                        s.sendMessage(tr("exempt-usage"));
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                minutes = Math.max(1, Math.min(minutes, 1440));
                plugin.setExempt(t, minutes * 60_000L);
                s.sendMessage(tr("exempt-on", "player", t.getName(), "minutes", minutes));
            }
            case "stats" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                s.sendMessage(tr("stats-title"));
                List<Map.Entry<String, Long>> rows = new ArrayList<>(plugin.violations().flagTotals().entrySet());
                rows.sort(Map.Entry.<String, Long>comparingByValue().reversed());
                if (rows.isEmpty()) s.sendMessage(tr("stats-none"));
                for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(10, rows.size()); i++) {
                    s.sendMessage(tr("stats-line", "check", rows.get(i).getKey(), "count", rows.get(i).getValue()));
                }
                s.sendMessage(tr("stats-punishments", "count", plugin.violations().punishTotal()));
            }
            case "reset" -> {
                if (!admin(s)) return true;
                Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return true;
                plugin.violations().reset(t.getUniqueId());
                s.sendMessage(tr("reset-done", "player", t.getName()));
            }
            default -> sendHelp(s);
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void dump(CommandSender s, String[] args) {
        Player t = findPlayer(s, args); if (t == null) return;
        try {
            Path dir = plugin.getDataFolder().toPath().resolve("reports");
            Files.createDirectories(dir);
            String stamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(FILE_TIME);
            Path file = dir.resolve(t.getName() + "-" + stamp + ".txt");
            Files.writeString(file, buildReport(t), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            s.sendMessage(tr("dump-saved", "file", plugin.getDataFolder().toPath().relativize(file)));
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning(plugin.lang().plain("warn-dump", "error", String.valueOf(ex.getMessage())));
            s.sendMessage(tr("dump-failed"));
        }
    }

    private String buildReport(Player p) {
        Location l = p.getLocation();
        Lang lg = plugin.lang();
        String none = lg.plain("dump-none");
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-header")).append('\n')
                .append(lg.plain("dump-generated", "value", LocalDateTime.now())).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-name", "value", p.getName())).append('\n')
                .append(lg.plain("dump-uuid", "value", p.getUniqueId())).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-ping", "value", Compat.ping(p))).append('\n')
                .append(lg.plain("dump-gamemode", "value", p.getGameMode())).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-world", "value", p.getWorld().getName())).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-position", "x", Lang.n(l.getX(), 3), "y", Lang.n(l.getY(), 3), "z", Lang.n(l.getZ(), 3))).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-health", "value", Lang.n(p.getHealth(), 1))).append('\n')
                .append(lg.plain("dump-food", "value", p.getFoodLevel())).append('\n');
        out.append('\n').append(lg.plain("dump-vl-title")).append('\n');
        Map<String, Double> vl = plugin.violations().get(p.getUniqueId());
        if (vl.isEmpty()) out.append(lg.plain("dump-no-vl")).append('\n');
        else vl.entrySet().stream().sorted(Map.Entry.<String, Double>comparingByValue().reversed())
                .forEach(e -> out.append(e.getKey()).append(": ").append(Lang.n(e.getValue(), 2)).append('\n'));
        out.append('\n').append(lg.plain("dump-flags-title")).append('\n');
        java.util.List<String> hist = plugin.violations().history(p.getUniqueId());
        if (hist.isEmpty()) out.append(none).append('\n');
        for (String line : hist) out.append(line).append('\n');
        out.append('\n').append(lg.plain("dump-reports-title")).append('\n');
        java.util.List<ReportManager.Report> rs = plugin.reports().openFor(p.getName());
        if (rs.isEmpty()) out.append(none).append('\n');
        for (ReportManager.Report r : rs) out.append(lg.plain("dump-report-line", "id", r.id,
                "reporter", r.reporterName, "reason", r.reason, "snapshot", r.snapshot)).append('\n');
        out.append('\n').append(lg.plain("dump-diag-title")).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-calls", "value", plugin.checks().calls())).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-avg", "value", Lang.n(plugin.checks().averageMicros(), 2))).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-max", "value", Lang.n(plugin.checks().maxMicros(), 2))).append('\n');
        out.append(lg.plain("dump-broken", "value", plugin.checks().brokenChecks())).append('\n');
        return out.toString();
    }

    private void sendHelp(CommandSender s) {
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-title", "version", plugin.getDescription().getVersion(), "author", plugin.authors()));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-alerts"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-info"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-inspect"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-report"));
        if (s.hasPermission("guardx.admin")) {
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-reload"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-debug"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-reports"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-close"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-history"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-dump"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-goto"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-exempt"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-stats"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-layers"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-notifytest"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-netcheck"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-netrevoke"));
            s.sendMessage(tr("help-netlist"));
        }
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-top"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-reset"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-diagnostics"));
        s.sendMessage(tr("help-checks"));
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> onTabComplete(CommandSender s, Command cmd, String alias, String[] args) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        if (cmd.getName().equalsIgnoreCase("report")) {
            String[] shifted = new String[args.length + 1];
            shifted[0] = "report";
            System.arraycopy(args, 0, shifted, 1, args.length);
            args = shifted;
        } else if (args.length == 1) {
            for (String sub : SUB) {
                if (!sub.startsWith(args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) continue;
                if (!sub.equals("report") && !sub.equals("help") && !sub.equals("alerts") && !s.hasPermission("guardx.admin")) continue;
                out.add(sub);
            }
            return out;
        }
        String first = args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (args.length == 2 && List.of("info", "reset", "inspect", "report", "history", "dump", "goto", "close", "exempt", "layers", "netcheck", "netrevoke").contains(first)) {
            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                if (p.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(args[1].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))
                        && !(first.equals("report") && s instanceof Player me && me.getUniqueId().equals(p.getUniqueId())))
                    out.add(p.getName());
            }
        } else if (args.length == 3 && first.equals("exempt")) {
            for (String o : List.of("off", "10", "30", "60")) if (o.startsWith(args[2].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) out.add(o);
        } else if (args.length == 3 && first.equals("report")) {
            for (String r : REASONS) if (r.startsWith(args[2].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) out.add(r);
        }
        return out;
    }
}

```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/GuardListener.java`:

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vunlior
 *
 * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
 */

package dev.guardx;

import dev.guardx.check.MoveContext;
import dev.guardx.data.PlayerData;
import dev.guardx.util.MoveUtil;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerChangedWorldEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.server.ServerCommandEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerRespawnEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerVelocityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityShootBowEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerItemConsumeEvent;

/** Собирает данные из событий сервера и передаёт их проверкам. */
public final class GuardListener implements Listener {

    private final GuardX plugin;

    public GuardListener(GuardX plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ движение

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent e) {
        Location from = e.getFrom();
        Location to = e.getTo();
        if (to == null || from.getWorld() == null || !from.getWorld().equals(to.getWorld())) return;

        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Запоминаем предыдущий и текущий поворот (нужны проверке хитбокса KillAura)
        d.prevYaw = d.yaw;
        d.prevPitch = d.pitch;
        d.yaw = to.getYaw();
        d.pitch = to.getPitch();

        boolean pos = from.getX() != to.getX() || from.getY() != to.getY() || from.getZ() != to.getZ();
        MoveContext c = new MoveContext(p, d, from, to, now, pos);

        if (pos) {
            c.serverGround = MoveUtil.onGround(to.getWorld(), to.getX(), to.getY(), to.getZ(), plugin.groundHeight());
            c.clientGround = p.isOnGround();
            if (MoveUtil.isIceUnder(to.getWorld(), to.getX(), to.getY(), to.getZ())) {
                d.lastIce = now;
            }
        }

        plugin.checks().handleMove(c);

        // Откат: возвращаем на последнюю безопасную позицию (или на предыдущую, если её нет)
        if (c.setback) {
            Location back = (d.lastSafe != null && to.getWorld().equals(d.lastSafe.getWorld()))
                    ? d.lastSafe.clone() : from.clone();
            back.setYaw(to.getYaw());
            back.setPitch(to.getPitch());
            d.ignoreTeleportUntil = now + 200;
            d.resetMovement();
            e.setTo(back);
            return;
        }

        if (!pos) return;

        // Обновляем состояние ПОСЛЕ проверок, чтобы они видели предыдущий тик
        if (c.serverGround || c.nearSpecial()) {
            d.airTicks = 0;
            d.groundY = to.getY();
        } else {
            d.airTicks++;
        }

        // Безопасной считаем позицию только пока игрок не "подозрителен"
        if (c.serverGround && !c.exempt() && !c.nearSpecial()
                && d.buffer("speed") < 1 && d.buffer("fly") < 1 && d.buffer("nofall") < 1) {
            d.lastSafe = to.clone();
        }
        d.lastDeltaY = c.dy;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent e) {
        if (e.getTo() == null) return;
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now <= d.ignoreTeleportUntil) return; // это наш собственный откат

        d.lastTeleport = now;
        d.lastSafe = e.getTo().clone();
        plugin.checks().handleTeleport(e.getPlayer(), d, e, now);
        d.resetMovement();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent e) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        d.lastTeleport = System.currentTimeMillis();
        d.lastSafe = e.getRespawnLocation().clone();
        d.resetMovement();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onWorldChange(PlayerChangedWorldEvent e) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        d.lastTeleport = System.currentTimeMillis();
        d.lastSafe = e.getPlayer().getLocation().clone();
        d.resetMovement();
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent e) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        d.lastVelocity = now;
        if (!e.isCancelled()) {
            plugin.checks().handleVelocity(e.getPlayer(), d, e.getVelocity(), now);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent e) {
        if (e.getEntity() instanceof Player p) {
            plugin.data(p).lastDamage = System.currentTimeMillis();
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ бой

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getDamager() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (e.getCause() != EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.ENTITY_ATTACK) return;

        Entity target = e.getEntity();
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(p);
        if (plugin.checks().handleAttack(p, d, target, System.currentTimeMillis())) {
            e.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ блоки

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDig(BlockDamageEvent e) {
        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        d.lastDig = now;
        d.lastDigKey = MoveUtil.blockKey(e.getBlock());
        if (plugin.checks().handleDig(p, d, e.getBlock(), now)) {
            e.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onBreak(BlockBreakEvent e) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        d.lastDig = now;
        if (!e.isCancelled()) plugin.checks().handleBreak(e.getPlayer(), d, e, now);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent e) {
        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        plugin.checks().handleInteract(p, plugin.data(p), e, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onShoot(EntityShootBowEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getEntity() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (plugin.checks().handleShoot(p, plugin.data(p), e, System.currentTimeMillis())) {
            e.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onConsume(PlayerItemConsumeEvent e) {
        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        if (plugin.checks().handleConsume(p, plugin.data(p), e, System.currentTimeMillis())) {
            e.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player p)) return;
        plugin.checks().handleInventoryClick(p, plugin.data(p), e, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onPlace(BlockPlaceEvent e) {
        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(p);
        if (plugin.checks().handlePlace(p, d, e.getBlockPlaced(), e.getBlockAgainst(), System.currentTimeMillis())) {
            e.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ вход / выход

    @EventHandler
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent e) {
        PlayerData d = plugin.data(e.getPlayer());
        d.lastSafe = e.getPlayer().getLocation().clone();
        d.yaw = d.prevYaw = e.getPlayer().getLocation().getYaw();
        d.pitch = d.prevPitch = e.getPlayer().getLocation().getPitch();
        if (plugin.packetLayer() != null) plugin.packetLayer().onJoin(e.getPlayer());
        plugin.network().onJoin(e.getPlayer());
        plugin.network().digestOnJoin(e.getPlayer());
    }

    /** Баны и разбаны администраторов для сети серверов. Нужно право guardx.network.ban, иначе любой игрок мог бы подделать запись. */
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onStaffCommand(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent e) {
        if (!e.getPlayer().hasPermission("guardx.network.ban")) return;
        plugin.network().onAdminCommand(e.getPlayer().getName(), e.getMessage());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onConsoleCommand(ServerCommandEvent e) {
        plugin.network().onAdminCommand("CONSOLE", e.getCommand());
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent e) {
        if (plugin.packetLayer() != null) plugin.packetLayer().onQuit(e.getPlayer());
        plugin.removeData(e.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        plugin.violations().clear(e.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
        plugin.reports().forget(e.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
    }
}

```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/GuardX.java`:

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vunlior
 *
 * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
 */

package dev.guardx;

import dev.guardx.check.CheckManager;
import dev.guardx.data.PlayerData;
import dev.guardx.hook.Hooks;
import dev.guardx.notify.Notifications;
import dev.guardx.packet.PacketLayer;
import dev.guardx.packet.PacketSettings;
import org.bstats.bukkit.Metrics;
import org.bstats.charts.SimplePie;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class GuardX extends JavaPlugin {

    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> data = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private ViolationManager violations;
    private ReportManager reports;
    private volatile boolean lowTps;
    private CheckManager checks;
    private Lang lang;
    private Notifications notifications;
    private HistoryStore history;
    private NetworkStore network;
    private LayerManager layers;
    private boolean placeholdersHooked;
    /** Пакетный слой: PacketEvents, если он установлен, иначе встроенный. Может быть не запущен (packets.backend: off). */
    private PacketLayer packets;
    private long enabledAtMs;
    private volatile PacketSettings settings;
    private volatile double groundHeight = 0.2;
    /** Растёт при каждой перезагрузке конфига: по нему проверки сбрасывают свой кэш настроек. */
    private volatile int configVersion;

    /**
     * Первый запуск: кладём config.yml на языке сервера (русский для ru_*, иначе английский).
     * Язык можно сменить потом: параметр language в конфиге и файлы в lang/.
     */
    private void installDefaultConfig() {
        java.io.File target = new java.io.File(getDataFolder(), "config.yml");
        if (target.exists()) return;
        boolean ru = "ru".equalsIgnoreCase(java.util.Locale.getDefault().getLanguage());
        if (!ru) {
            try (java.io.InputStream in = getResource("config_en.yml")) {
                if (in != null) {
                    getDataFolder().mkdirs();
                    java.nio.file.Files.copy(in, target.toPath());
                    return;
                }
            } catch (java.io.IOException ex) {
                getLogger().warning("config_en.yml: " + ex.getMessage());
            }
        }
        saveDefaultConfig();
    }

    @Override
    public void onEnable() {
        long startedAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
        installDefaultConfig();
        lang = new Lang(this);
        lang.load(getConfig().getString("language", "ru"));
        notifications = new Notifications(this);
        layers = new LayerManager(this);
        checkServerVersion();
        validateConfig();
        settings = new PacketSettings(getConfig());
        groundHeight = getConfig().getDouble("ground-height", 0.2);

        reports = new ReportManager(this);
        history = new HistoryStore(this);
        network = new NetworkStore(this);
        violations = new ViolationManager(this);
        checks = new CheckManager(this);

        getServer().getPluginManager().registerEvents(new GuardListener(this), this);

        // Пакетный слой: PacketEvents необязателен (softdepend). Нет его — работает встроенный слой на Netty.
        packets = new PacketLayer(this);
        packets.start();

        GuardCommand command = new GuardCommand(this);
        if (getCommand("guardx") != null) {
            getCommand("guardx").setExecutor(command);
            getCommand("guardx").setTabCompleter(command);
        }
        if (getCommand("report") != null) {
            getCommand("report").setExecutor(command);
            getCommand("report").setTabCompleter(command);
        }

        // каждый тик запоминаем хитбоксы игроков — для компенсации пинга в Reach и KillAura
        getServer().getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> {
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            for (Player p : getServer().getOnlinePlayers()) {
                PlayerData d = data(p);
                d.history.add(now, p.getBoundingBox());
                checks.handleTick(p, d, now);
            }
        }, 1L, 1L);

        // защита от ложных срабатываний при просадке TPS: пока сервер лагает, проверки движения не работают
        getServer().getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> {
            double min = getConfig().getDouble("lag-protect.min-tps", 16.0);
            if (min <= 0) { lowTps = false; return; }
            try {
                double[] tps = getServer().getTPS();
                lowTps = tps != null && tps.length > 0 && tps[0] < min;
            } catch (Throwable ex) {
                lowTps = false; // API недоступен (не Paper) — защиту пропускаем
            }
        }, 100L, 40L);

        // раз в 10 секунд снижаем VL
        getServer().getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> violations.decay(), 200L, 200L);

        placeholdersHooked = Hooks.registerPlaceholders(this);
        startMetrics();

        enabledAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
        printEnableBanner(enabledAtMs - startedAt);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisable() {
        int tracked = data.size();
        // Снимаем пакетный слой до очистки данных, чтобы он не продолжал вызывать наш код.
        if (packets != null) {
            packets.stop();
        }
        if (violations != null) violations.shutdown();
        if (reports != null) reports.shutdown();
        data.clear();
        if (notifications != null) notifications.shutdown();
        if (history != null) history.shutdown();
        if (network != null) network.shutdown();
        if (lang != null) printDisableBanner(tracked);
    }

    /** Анонимная статистика bStats (https://bstats.org/plugin/bukkit/GuardX/35139). Владелец сервера может отключить её в plugins/bStats/config.yml. */
    private void startMetrics() {
        try {
            Metrics metrics = new Metrics(this, 35139);
            metrics.addCustomChart(new SimplePie("language", () -> lang.code()));
            metrics.addCustomChart(new SimplePie("punishments",
                    () -> getConfig().getBoolean("punish.enabled", true) ? "enabled" : "observe-only"));
        } catch (Throwable ex) {
            getLogger().warning("bStats: " + ex);
        }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- красивый лог

    private void console(String msg) {
        getServer().getConsoleSender().sendMessage(msg);
    }

    public String authors() {
        var list = getDescription().getAuthors();
        return list.isEmpty() ? "Vunlior" : String.join(", ", list);
    }

    private void printEnableBanner(long tookMs) {
        PacketSettings st = settings;
        StringBuilder layers = new StringBuilder();
        if (st != null) {
            if (st.timer) layers.append("Timer, ");
            if (st.clicks) layers.append("AutoClicker, ");
            if (st.killaura) layers.append("KillAura, ");
            if (st.aim) layers.append("Aim, ");
            if (st.noSwing) layers.append("NoSwing, ");
            if (st.badPackets) layers.append("BadPackets, ");
        }
        String packetModules = layers.length() == 0 ? "-" : layers.substring(0, layers.length() - 2);

        StringBuilder hooks = new StringBuilder();
        if (placeholdersHooked) hooks.append("PlaceholderAPI, ");
        if (notifications != null) {
            for (var channel : notifications.channels()) {
                if (channel.enabled()) {
                    String n = channel.name();
                    hooks.append(Character.toUpperCase(n.charAt(0))).append(n.substring(1)).append(", ");
                }
            }
        }
        String hookList = hooks.length() == 0 ? "-" : hooks.substring(0, hooks.length() - 2);

        String punish = lang.tr(getConfig().getBoolean("punish.enabled", true) ? "banner-punish-on" : "banner-punish-off");

        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
        console(lang.tr("banner-enabled", "version", getDescription().getVersion()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
        console(lang.tr("banner-author", "value", authors()));
        String mcVersion;
        try {
            mcVersion = getServer().getMinecraftVersion(); // Paper 1.16.5+
        } catch (Throwable ex) {
            mcVersion = getServer().getBukkitVersion();
        }
        console(lang.tr("banner-server", "value", getServer().getName() + " " + mcVersion));
        console(lang.tr("banner-backend", "value", packets == null ? "-" : packets.name()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-checks", "value", checks.all().size()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-packet", "value", packetModules));
        console(lang.tr("banner-punish", "value", punish));
        console(lang.tr("banner-hooks", "value", hookList));
        console(lang.tr("banner-lang", "value", lang.code()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-startup", "value", tookMs));
        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
    }

    private void printDisableBanner(int trackedPlayers) {
        long uptime = enabledAtMs == 0 ? 0 : (System.currentTimeMillis() - enabledAtMs) / 1000;
        String time = lang.tr("uptime-format", "h", uptime / 3600,
                "m", String.format("%02d", (uptime % 3600) / 60), "s", String.format("%02d", uptime % 60));
        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
        console(lang.tr("banner-disabled", "version", getDescription().getVersion()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
        console(lang.tr("banner-uptime", "value", time));
        console(lang.tr("banner-cleared", "value", trackedPlayers));
        console(lang.tr("banner-author", "value", authors()));
        console(lang.tr("banner-line"));
    }

    public void reloadAll() {
        reloadConfig();
        configVersion++;
        lang.load(getConfig().getString("language", "ru"));
        layers.reload();
        if (network != null) network.reconnect();
        validateConfig();
        settings = new PacketSettings(getConfig());
        groundHeight = getConfig().getDouble("ground-height", 0.2);
    }

    /** Поддерживаются версии Minecraft 1.16 — 1.21.x. На остальных работаем, но предупреждаем: пороги там не проверялись. */
    private void checkServerVersion() {
        String v;
        try {
            v = getServer().getMinecraftVersion();
        } catch (Throwable ex) {
            v = getServer().getBukkitVersion();
        }
        if (v == null || !v.matches("1\\.(1[6-9]|2[01])(\\..*)?(-.*)?")) {
            getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-unsupported-version", "version", String.valueOf(v)));
        }
    }

    /** Проверяет опасные/некорректные значения конфигурации и сообщает о них без изменения наказаний. */
    private void validateConfig() {
        double decay = getConfig().getDouble("vl-decay-per-10s", 1.0);
        if (!Double.isFinite(decay) || decay < 0) {
            getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-decay"));
        }
        double globalVl = getConfig().getDouble("punish.vl", 25.0);
        if (!Double.isFinite(globalVl) || globalVl <= 0) {
            getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-punish-vl"));
        }
        if (getConfig().getBoolean("checks.fastbreak.enabled", false)) {
            getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-fastbreak"));
        }
        // Сеть серверов: типичные ошибки конфигурации, которые молча ломают общий бан-лист
        if (getConfig().getBoolean("network.enabled", false)) {
            String id = getConfig().getString("network.server-id", "server-1");
            if (id == null || id.isBlank() || id.trim().equals("server-1")) {
                getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-network-server-id"));
            }
            if (getConfig().getBoolean("network.ip-match", true)
                    && getConfig().getString("network.ip-salt", "").isBlank()) {
                getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-network-salt"));
            }
            if (!getConfig().getBoolean("network.storage.use-ssl", false)
                    && !"sqlite".equalsIgnoreCase(getConfig().getString("network.storage.type", "mysql"))) {
                getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-network-ssl"));
            }
            String storage = getConfig().getString("network.storage.type", "mysql");
            if (!"sqlite".equalsIgnoreCase(storage)
                    && getConfig().getString("network.storage.password", "").isBlank()) {
                getLogger().warning(lang.plain("warn-network-password"));
            }
        }
    }

    public int configVersion() {
        return configVersion;
    }

    /** TPS сервера (1 минута). Вне Paper метода нет — тогда считаем, что сервер не лагает. */
    public double tps() {
        try {
            double[] t = getServer().getTPS();
            return t != null && t.length > 0 ? t[0] : 20.0;
        } catch (Throwable ex) {
            return 20.0;
        }
    }

    /** Игрок не проверяется: право guardx.bypass или временное исключение (/guardx exempt). */
    public boolean bypass(Player p) {
        if (p.hasPermission("guardx.bypass")) return true;
        PlayerData d = data.get(p.getUniqueId());
        return d != null && d.exemptUntil > System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    /** Временно (millis > 0) или снова (millis <= 0) проверять игрока: /guardx exempt и GuardAPI. */
    public void setExempt(Player p, long millis) {
        data(p).exemptUntil = millis > 0 ? System.currentTimeMillis() + millis : 0L;
    }

    /**
     * Нужно ли вообще учитывать нарушение {@code check} этого игрока: не обход, не наблюдатель, не отключено слоем.
     * Общий шлюз для нарушений из сетевого потока и из API.
     */
    public boolean shouldCheck(Player p, String check) {
        return !bypass(p) && p.getGameMode() != GameMode.SPECTATOR && !layers.skips(p, check);
    }

    public PlayerData data(Player p) {
        return data.computeIfAbsent(p.getUniqueId(), PlayerData::new);
    }

    /** Данные игрока, если они уже есть. Безопасно вызывать из сетевого потока. */
    public PlayerData peekData(UUID id) {
        return data.get(id);
    }

    public void removeData(UUID id) {
        data.remove(id);
        if (packets != null) packets.forget(id);
    }

    /** Пакетный слой (может быть не запущен). Нужен слушателю входа/выхода игроков. */
    public PacketLayer packetLayer() {
        return packets;
    }

    public Lang lang() {
        return lang;
    }

    public Notifications notifications() {
        return notifications;
    }

    public HistoryStore history() {
        return history;
    }

    public NetworkStore network() {
        return network;
    }

    public LayerManager layers() {
        return layers;
    }

    public ViolationManager violations() {
        return violations;
    }

    public ReportManager reports() {
        return reports;
    }

    /** Сервер сейчас сильно лагает: проверки движения и боя пропускаются, чтобы не наказывать за лаги. */
    public boolean lowTps() {
        return lowTps;
    }

    public CheckManager checks() {
        return checks;
    }

    public double groundHeight() {
        return groundHeight;
    }

    public PacketSettings settings() {
        return settings;
    }

    /**
     * Нарушение, найденное в сетевом потоке. Всё, что трогает игрока или мир,
     * выполняется уже в основном потоке сервера.
     */
    public void flagAsync(UUID id, String check, String info, double vl, boolean setback) {
        if (!isEnabled()) return;
        try {
            getServer().getScheduler().runTask(this, () -> {
                Player p = getServer().getPlayer(id);
                if (p == null || !shouldCheck(p, check)) return;

                PlayerData d = data(p);
                violations.flag(p, d, check, info, vl);

                if (setback && getConfig().getBoolean("setback", true)) {
                    Location safe = d.lastSafe;
                    if (safe != null && safe.getWorld() != null && safe.getWorld().equals(p.getWorld())) {
                        Location back = safe.clone();
                        back.setYaw(p.getLocation().getYaw());
                        back.setPitch(p.getLocation().getPitch());
                        d.ignoreTeleportUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 200;
                        d.resetMovement();
                        p.teleport(back);
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (RuntimeException ignored) {
            // плагин выключается — нарушение уже не важно
        }
    }
}

```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/HistoryStore.java`:

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vunlior
 *
 * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
 */

package dev.guardx;

import dev.guardx.api.GuardHistoryEntry;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

/**
 * Единственное место, где живёт история нарушений игроков.
 * <ul>
 *   <li>последние записи online-игроков держим в памяти (быстрый доступ для /guardx dump и оповещений);</li>
 *   <li>если history.persist включён — каждая запись дописывается в plugins/GuardX/history/&lt;uuid&gt;.tsv,
 *       поэтому историю можно смотреть после перезапуска и у игроков, которых сейчас нет на сервере.</li>
 * </ul>
 * Всё чтение и запись файлов идёт в одном фоновом потоке, поэтому порядок записей сохраняется.
 */
public final class HistoryStore {

    private static final int CACHE_SIZE = 30;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIME =
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss").withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault());

    private final GuardX plugin;
    private final Path dir;
    private final Path namesFile;
    private final Map<UUID, Deque<GuardHistoryEntry>> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    /** ник (в нижнем регистре) -> UUID: нужен, чтобы смотреть историю тех, кого нет на сервере. */
    private final Map<String, UUID> names = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final ExecutorService io = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> {
        Thread t = new Thread(r, "GuardX-History");
        t.setDaemon(true);
        return t;
    });

    public HistoryStore(GuardX plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        this.dir = plugin.getDataFolder().toPath().resolve("history");
        this.namesFile = dir.resolve("names.tsv");
        if (persist()) {
            try {
                Files.createDirectories(dir);
            } catch (IOException ex) {
                warn(ex);
            }
            loadNames();
            submit(this::cleanup);
        }
    }

    public boolean persist() {
        return plugin.getConfig().getBoolean("history.persist", true);
    }

    /** Сколько записей максимум хранится на одного игрока. */
    public int maxEntries() {
        return Math.max(10, plugin.getConfig().getInt("history.max-entries", 500));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ запись

    public void add(Player p, String check, String info, double total) {
        UUID id = p.getUniqueId();
        GuardHistoryEntry e = new GuardHistoryEntry(System.currentTimeMillis(), check, total, info);
        Deque<GuardHistoryEntry> q = cache.computeIfAbsent(id, k -> new ArrayDeque<>());
        synchronized (q) {
            q.addLast(e);
            while (q.size() > CACHE_SIZE) q.pollFirst();
        }
        if (!persist()) return;

        String key = p.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!id.equals(names.put(key, id))) {
            String row = key + "\t" + id + "\n";
            submit(() -> append(namesFile, row));
        }
        String line = e.time() + "\t" + check + "\t" + String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", total) + "\t" + clean(info) + "\n";
        submit(() -> append(dir.resolve(id + ".tsv"), line));
    }

    /** Игрок вышел: освобождаем память и при необходимости подрезаем файл. */
    public void unload(UUID id) {
        cache.remove(id);
        if (persist()) submit(() -> trim(id));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ чтение

    /** Последние записи из памяти (только для игроков, которые сейчас на сервере). Старые — первыми. */
    public List<GuardHistoryEntry> recent(UUID id) {
        Deque<GuardHistoryEntry> q = cache.get(id);
        if (q == null) return List.of();
        synchronized (q) {
            return new ArrayList<>(q);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Записи игрока из файла (до {@code limit} последних, старые первыми).
     * Блокирует вызывающий поток на время чтения файла (обычно миллисекунды), поэтому в игровом тике зови редко.
     */
    public List<GuardHistoryEntry> entries(UUID id, int limit) {
        if (!persist()) return tail(recent(id), limit);
        try {
            Future<List<GuardHistoryEntry>> f = io.submit(() -> readTail(id, limit));
            return f.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return tail(recent(id), limit);
        }
    }

    /** UUID игрока по нику из индекса истории (null — такого игрока в истории нет). */
    public UUID find(String name) {
        return name == null ? null : names.get(name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
    }

    /** Ник по UUID из индекса (null — неизвестен). */
    public String nameOf(UUID id) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, UUID> en : names.entrySet()) {
            if (en.getValue().equals(id)) return en.getKey();
        }
        return null;
    }

    /** Строка истории для консоли и файла-отчёта. */
    public static String line(GuardHistoryEntry e) {
        return "[" + TIME.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(e.time())) + "] " + e.check() + " " + e.info()
                + " VL=" + String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", e.vl());
    }

    public static String time(GuardHistoryEntry e) {
        return TIME.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(e.time()));
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        io.shutdown();
        try {
            io.awaitTermination(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException ex) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ работа с файлами (поток GuardX-History)

    private void submit(Runnable r) {
        try {
            io.execute(r);
        } catch (RejectedExecutionException ignored) {
            // плагин выключается
        }
    }

    private static List<GuardHistoryEntry> tail(List<GuardHistoryEntry> all, int limit) {
        return all.size() <= limit ? all : new ArrayList<>(all.subList(all.size() - limit, all.size()));
    }

    private static String clean(String s) {
        return s == null ? "" : s.replace('\t', ' ').replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ');
    }

    private void append(Path file, String text) {
        try {
            Files.writeString(file, text, StandardCharsets.UTF_8, StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            warn(ex);
        }
    }

    private List<GuardHistoryEntry> readTail(UUID id, int limit) throws IOException {
        Path file = dir.resolve(id + ".tsv");
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) return List.of();
        List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        List<GuardHistoryEntry> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = Math.max(0, lines.size() - limit); i < lines.size(); i++) {
            GuardHistoryEntry e = parse(lines.get(i));
            if (e != null) out.add(e);
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static GuardHistoryEntry parse(String line) {
        String[] p = line.split("\t", 4);
        if (p.length < 4) return null;
        try {
            return new GuardHistoryEntry(Long.parseLong(p[0]), p[1], Double.parseDouble(p[2]), p[3]);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Оставляет в файле только последние history.max-entries записей (когда он заметно вырос). */
    private void trim(UUID id) {
        Path file = dir.resolve(id + ".tsv");
        try {
            int max = maxEntries();
            if (!Files.isRegularFile(file) || Files.size(file) < max * 160L) return;
            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            if (lines.size() <= max) return;
            Path tmp = dir.resolve(id + ".tmp");
            Files.write(tmp, lines.subList(lines.size() - max, lines.size()), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            Files.move(tmp, file, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            warn(ex);
        }
    }

    private void loadNames() {
        try {
            if (!Files.isRegularFile(namesFile)) return;
            for (String line : Files.readAllLines(namesFile, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                String[] p = line.split("\t");
                if (p.length != 2) continue;
                try {
                    names.put(p[0], UUID.fromString(p[1]));
                } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                    // битая строка — пропускаем
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            warn(ex);
        }
    }

    /** Удаляет файлы игроков, у которых нет новых нарушений дольше history.retention-days (0 = не удалять). */
    private void cleanup() {
        int days = plugin.getConfig().getInt("history.retention-days", 30);
        if (days <= 0) return;
        long limit = System.currentTimeMillis() - days * 86_400_000L;
        try (Stream<Path> files = Files.list(dir)) {
            files.filter(f -> f.getFileName().toString().endsWith(".tsv") && !f.equals(namesFile)).forEach(f -> {
                try {
                    if (Files.getLastModifiedTime(f).toMillis() < limit) Files.deleteIfExists(f);
                } catch (IOException ex) {
                    warn(ex);
                }
            });
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            warn(ex);
        }
    }

    private void warn(IOException ex) {
        plugin.getLogger().warning(plugin.lang().plain("warn-history-io", "error", String.valueOf(ex.getMessage())));
    }
}
```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/Lang.java`:

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vunlior
 *
 * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
 */

package dev.guardx;

import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Языковые файлы: plugins/GuardX/lang/ru.yml и en.yml (создаются при первом запуске, можно править).
 * Порядок поиска ключа: файл на диске -> встроенный файл выбранного языка -> встроенный английский -> сам ключ.
 */
public final class Lang {

    private final GuardX plugin;
    private YamlConfiguration disk;
    private YamlConfiguration bundled;
    private YamlConfiguration english;
    private String code = "ru";

    public Lang(GuardX plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public void load(String language) {
        String c = language == null ? "ru" : language.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        code = c.matches("[a-z]{2,8}") ? c : "ru";

        english = bundled("en");
        bundled = bundled(code);
        if (bundled == null) bundled = english;

        for (String name : new String[]{"ru", "en"}) {
            File f = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "lang/" + name + ".yml");
            if (!f.exists()) {
                try {
                    plugin.saveResource("lang/" + name + ".yml", false);
                } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                    // файла нет в jar — не страшно
                }
            }
        }

        disk = null;
        File f = new File(plugin.getDataFolder(), "lang/" + code + ".yml");
        if (f.isFile()) {
            try (InputStreamReader r = new InputStreamReader(new java.io.FileInputStream(f), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                disk = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(r);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("lang/" + code + ".yml: " + ex.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    private YamlConfiguration bundled(String name) {
        try (InputStream in = plugin.getResource("lang/" + name + ".yml")) {
            if (in == null) return null;
            return YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Число с фиксированным числом знаков, всегда с точкой (не зависит от локали сервера). */
    public static String n(double v, int decimals) {
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%." + decimals + "f", v);
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }

    private String raw(String key) {
        String s = disk == null ? null : disk.getString(key);
        if (s == null && bundled != null) s = bundled.getString(key);
        if (s == null && english != null) s = english.getString(key);
        return s == null ? key : s;
    }

    /** Текст с цветами. Пары kv: "имя", значение, ... Значения подставляются ПОСЛЕ перевода цветов (чужой текст не красится). */
    public String tr(String key, Object... kv) {
        String s = ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', raw(key));
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < kv.length; i += 2) {
            s = s.replace("{" + kv[i] + "}", String.valueOf(kv[i + 1]));
        }
        return s;
    }

    /** То же, но с префиксом [GuardX] в начале. */
    public String msg(String key, Object... kv) {
        return tr("prefix") + tr(key, kv);
    }

    /** Без цветов (для логов и Discord). */
    public String plain(String key, Object... kv) {
        return ChatColor.stripColor(tr(key, kv));
    }
}
```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/LayerManager.java`:

```java
/*
 * Copyright 2026 Vunlior
 *
 * Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 * you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
 */

package dev.guardx;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * «Слои» — правила, по которым часть проверок пропускается для игрока в нужном месте или с нужным правом.
 * Описываются в config.yml (раздел layers). Слой действует, когда выполнены ВСЕ его условия:
 * мир (если задан), зона (если задана) и право (если задано).
 * Любая проверка, которую слой перечисляет в checks (или "*" — все), для игрока пропускается целиком:
 * не считается, не отменяет действия, не даёт VL и не наказывает.
 * Проверки (и встроенные, и добавленные через API) спрашивают слои в одном месте — CheckManager / GuardX#shouldCheck.
 */
public final class LayerManager {

    /** Один слой. {@code worlds} пустой = любой мир; {@code min}/{@code max} null = без зоны. */
    public record Layer(String name, Set<String> worlds, double[] min, double[] max,
                        Set<String> checks, String permission) {

        public boolean allChecks() {
            return checks.contains("*");
        }

        boolean covers(Player p, String check) {
            if (!allChecks() && !checks.contains(check)) return false;
            if (!worlds.isEmpty() && !worlds.contains(p.getWorld().getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) return false;
            if (permission != null && !p.hasPermission(permission)) return false;
            if (min != null) {
                Location l = p.getLocation();
                return l.getX() >= min[0] && l.getX() <= max[0]
                        && l.getY() >= min[1] && l.getY() <= max[1]
                        && l.getZ() >= min[2] && l.getZ() <= max[2];
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    private final GuardX plugin;
    private volatile List<Layer> layers = List.of();

    public LayerManager(GuardX plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        reload();
    }

    /** Читает раздел layers из конфига (вызывается при запуске и /guardx reload). */
    public void reload() {
        List<Layer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        int index = 0;
        for (Map<?, ?> raw : plugin.getConfig().getMapList("layers")) {
            index++;
            try {
                out.add(parse(raw, index));
            } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
                plugin.getLogger().warning(plugin.lang().plain("warn-layer", "index", index, "error", String.valueOf(ex.getMessage())));
            }
        }
        layers = List.copyOf(out);
    }

    private static Layer parse(Map<?, ?> m, int index) {
        String name = m.get("name") == null ? "layer-" + index : String.valueOf(m.get("name"));

        Set<String> worlds = new HashSet<>();
        for (String w : strings(m.get("worlds"))) worlds.add(w.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));

        Set<String> checks = new HashSet<>();
        for (String c : strings(m.get("checks"))) checks.add(c.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        if (checks.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("checks is empty");

        double[] a = coords(m.get("min"));
        double[] b = coords(m.get("max"));
        if ((a == null) != (b == null)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("min and max must be set together");
        double[] min = null;
        double[] max = null;
        if (a != null) {
            min = new double[]{Math.min(a[0], b[0]), Math.min(a[1], b[1]), Math.min(a[2], b[2])};
            max = new double[]{Math.max(a[0], b[0]), Math.max(a[1], b[1]), Math.max(a[2], b[2])};
        }
        Object perm = m.get("permission");
        String permission = perm == null || String.valueOf(perm).isBlank() ? null : String.valueOf(perm).trim();
        return new Layer(name, Set.copyOf(worlds), min, max, Set.copyOf(checks), permission);
    }

    private static List<String> strings(Object o) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        if (o instanceof List<?> list) {
            for (Object e : list) if (e != null) out.add(String.valueOf(e).trim());
        } else if (o != null) {
            out.add(String.valueOf(o).trim());
        }
        out.removeIf(String::isEmpty);
        return out;
    }

    private static double[] coords(Object o) {
        if (o == null) return null;
        if (!(o instanceof List<?> l) || l.size() != 3) throw new IllegalArgumentException("coordinates must be [x, y, z]");
        double[] r = new double[3];
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            if (!(l.get(i) instanceof Number n)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("coordinates must be numbers");
            r[i] = n.doubleValue();
        }
        return r;
    }

    /** Пропускать ли проверку {@code check} для игрока сейчас. Без слоёв — мгновенно false. */
    public boolean skips(Player p, String check) {
        List<Layer> ls = layers;
        if (ls.isEmpty()) return false;
        for (Layer l : ls) if (l.covers(p, check)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    public List<Layer> all() {
        return layers;
    }

    /** Слои, которые сейчас действуют на игрока (хотя бы для одной проверки). */
    public List<Layer> activeFor(Player p) {
        List<Layer> out = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Layer l : layers) {
            if (l.allChecks() ? l.covers(p, "*") : l.checks().stream().anyMatch(c -> l.covers(p, c))) out.add(l);
        }
        return out;
    }
}

```

`GuardX-source/GuardX/src/main/java/dev/guardx/NetworkStore.java`:

```java
/*

 * You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *

 *
 * Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 * limitations under the License.
```
